# Audit report

Made by scripts/audit-report.cjs from audit/results. Rules and file format: docs/AUDIT.md.

- Films checked: 561 of 900
- Lines checked and right: 7888
- Wrong lines still open: 21 (fixed: 547)
- Unsure lines still open: 535
- Average vibe: 3.51 out of 5
- Flat menus (vibe under 3.5) waiting for Josh's call: 283

| Rosemary's Baby (1968) | hotel | 4 | 4 | 4 | 4 | 4 | 4 | The hotel room service frame suits the Bramford, the mousse and tannis root are used well. | Terry's fall and her tannis root pendant; Hutch's coma and the anagram book; Dr Sapirstein's tannis root smell; Minnie's health drink poured down the sink; Rosemary's short haircut; The Pope's visit on TV | Swap 'Minnie's herbal tonic shots' note for 'Poured down the sink'.; Rename 'Tannis root tea' to 'Terry's pendant tea'.; Swap the 'Vodka gimlet' note for the real party reference.; Add Hutch's anagram clue to the hang tag. |
| Star Wars: The Force Awakens (2015) | bartab | 5 | 4 | 4 | 4 | 3 | 4 | Maz's castle bar tab is a great setting and the Jakku portion slip is a proper nod, but the second face leans on a Leia photo and the look is plain. | Rey eating portion bread inside Hellhound Two on Jakku; Maz taking off her goggles to tell Rey the truth; Lor San Tekka placing the map in Poe's hand; Rey's Jedi mind trick on the stormtrooper; Han and Ben on the Starkiller Base walkway; The vault where Rey finds Luke's lightsaber | Swap the Leia photo on face two for a still of Maz's castle bar.; Replace the 'Crispy pork belly bites' note with Maz: 'I am no Jedi, but I know the Force.'; Swap the 'BB-8 saffron arancini' note for 'Poe's map, hidden in a droid.'; Rename 'Maz Kanata's slow-roast lamb feast' to 'The vault lamb' with a Luke's lightsaber nod. |
| The Babadook (2014) | cafe | 4 | 4 | 4 | 4 | 4 | 4 | A strong one, with fairy bread, Milo and the pop-up book all in the right Australian suburban gloom. | Worms in a bowl taken down to the basement (the ending); Glass found in Amelia's food (the middle of the film); Dook dook dook at the door (the knocks); Amelia unravelling after no sleep (the middle of the film); Samuel's birthday cake at the end (the finale); The black ooze she throws up (the basement) | Swap 'Midnight cheese toasties' for 'Basement worms, jelly in a bowl' with the ending as the note.; Swap the made up Amelia note for her real house line.; Swap 'Pop-up book crackers and olive tapenade' for 'Mister Babadook, if it's in a word or it's in a look' as a shared plate.; Add a glass shard gag to 'Amelia's slow-cooked stew' note. |
| The Incredibles (2004) | placemat | 4 | 4 | 4 | 4 | 4 | 4 | A good family placemat with Edna, Frozone and Jack-Jack all in, though a few dish notes are invented. | No capes (Edna Mode); Honey, where is my super suit (Frozone); Dash running across the water (Nomanisan Island); The Parrs' first dinner table row (Bob and Helen); Jack-Jack's powers in the nursery (the baby sitter); Syndrome's monologue, and I'll tell you a secret (Nomanisan Island) | Swap the made up Edna note for her real never look back line.; Swap 'Crispy roast potatoes with rosemary salt' for 'No capes, no gravy boat' to use the famous Edna line.; Swap 'Mirage's Nomanisan Island steak dinner' for 'Bob's briefing breakfast on the island'.; Swap 'Strawberry milk' for 'Frozone's Where is my suit? punch' with the missing suit gag. |
| The Matrix (1999) | galley | 4 | 4 | 4 | 4 | 4 | 4 | A strong one, with Cypher's steak, the Oracle's cookies and the pill fizz all used well. | Tasty Wheat gruel and Mouse's riff on what it tastes like (the cue); I know this steak doesn't exist, ignorance is bliss (Cypher and Agent Smith); There is no spoon (the Oracle's waiting room); I know kung fu (the training program); Wake up, Neo, the Matrix has you (Neo's screen); Dodging the bullets on the roof (the lobby and roof) | Swap the made up Morpheus note for his real red pill line.; Swap 'Neo's street noodles' for 'Mouse's Tasty Wheat, tastes like what you think it tastes like'.; Swap 'Steamed edamame with sea salt' note for a spoon gag with 'There is no spoon' printed under it.; Add 'Wake up, Neo' as a green rain line behind the title. |

(Dug, in the jungle); Fentons ice cream and the curb where Russell counts cars with his dad; Ellie's scrapbook page 'Thanks for the adventure' (Carl, after Kevin is lost); Russell's snipe hunt under the porch (Russell, before take off); Muntz's dinner table with the talking dogs (Muntz's airship) | Swap 'Grape soda badge jellies' for 'Fentons chocolate and butter-brickle scoops' to match the closing kerb cue; Replace the generic note heading 'A note from Russell' text with the butter-brickle line so the cue and the note tell one story; Swap 'Cheese and tomato toasties' for 'Wilderness Explorer snipe sandwiches' (a joke on Russell's snipe hunt) |
